17

The King shall come
When morning dawns
And light triumphant breaks,
When beauty gilds the eastern hills
And life to joy awakes.

THE KING SHALL COME
Verse 1
18

Refrain:
Come, O come, King Jesus
Come, O come, Emmanuel!
Come quickly, Lord, 
Thy church doth wait.
O come, Emmanuel! 
Come, Emmanuel!

THE KING SHALL COME
Refrain
19

Not as of old a little child
To bear and fight and die,
But crowned with glory as the sun
That lights the morning sky.

THE KING SHALL COME

20

Refrain:
Come, O come, King Jesus
Come, O come, Emmanuel!
Come quickly, Lord, 
Thy church doth wait.
O come, Emmanuel! 
Come, Emmanuel!

When morning dawns
And light and beauty brings.

Come quickly, King of kings!

60

Amazing grace, how sweet the sound 
That saved a wretch like me.
I once was lost, but now am found,
Was blind, but now I see.

AMAZING GRACE
Verse 1
61

‘Twas grace that taught my heart to fear
And grace my fears relieved.
How precious did that grace appear 
The hour I first believed.
AMAZING GRACE
Verse 2
62

Through many dangers, toils and snares
I have already come.
‘Tis grace has brought me safe thus far
And grace will lead me home.
AMAZING GRACE
Verse 3
63

The Lord has promised good to me,
His word my hope secures.
He will my shield and portion be
As long as life endures.
AMAZING GRACE
Verse 4
64

And when this flesh and heart shall fail
And mortal life shall cease,
I shall possess within the veil
A life of joy and peace.
AMAZING GRACE
Verse 5
65

When we’ve been there 
Ten thousand years,
Bright shining as the sun.
We’ve no less days to sing God’s praise
Than when we’d first begun.

In all His words and deeds.
His steadfast love is 
For all who call upon His name.

That He will send His Spirit,
And make His dwelling in our midst.

And the Word was God.

and without Him nothing was made
that has been made.

1815

(Asst.)  In Him was life,
And the life was the light of men.
(Group)  The light shines in the darkness, 
and the darkness has not overcome it.
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1816

(Asst.)  Heavenly Father, in honor of Your Son, Light of the World and Author of Life, we are about to kindle the light for the Lord’s Day.  On this day You raised Your Son, Jesus, from the dead, and began the new creation.
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1817

May our celebration of His resurrection this day be filled with Your peace and heavenly blessing.  Be gracious to us and cause Your Holy Spirit to dwell more richly among us. 
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1818

Father of mercy, continue Your loving kindness toward us.  Make us worthy to walk in the way of Your Son, loyal to Your teaching, and unwavering in love and service.
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1819

Keep far from us all anxiety, darkness and gloom; and grant that peace, light and joy ever abide among us.
(Group)  For in You is the fountain of life; 
In Your light do we see light.

(Leader)  Blessed are you, Lord our God, for by your great mercy we have been born anew to a living hope through the resurrection of Jesus Christ from the dead, and to an inheritance 
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1857

that is imperishable, undefiled and unfading.  Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the Universe, for giving us new life in Your Son.


(Group)  Blessed be His name forever.


CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1858

(Leader)  Have mercy, Lord our God, upon Your people who belong to Your Son, the dwelling place of Your Spirit.  Grant that the Christian people throughout the world may attain the unity for which Jesus prayed
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1859

on the eve of his sacrifice, and that we in 
Ang Lingkod ng Panginoon
may be a sign of that unity and a means of its growth.  May all your people be renewed in the power of your Spirit, 

CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1860

so that we might be without spot or blemish, and ready for your Son’s return.  Blessed are you, Lord our God, King of the Universe, Ruler and Builder of your people.

(Group)  Blessed be His name forever.


CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1861

(Leader)  May the Lord bless you and keep you; may the Lord make his face to shine upon you and be gracious to you; may the Lord lift up his countenance upon you and give you peace.

(Group)  Amen.

CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1863

Blessing Before the Meal 

(Leader)  Blessed be the Lord God of Israel,
for he has visited and redeemed his people,
and has raised up a horn of salvation for us
in the house of his servant David,
as he spoke by the mouth of his holy prophets from of old,
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1864

that we should be saved from our enemies,
and from the hand of all who hate us;
to perform the mercy promised to our fathers,
and to remember his holy covenant,
the oath which he swore to our father Abraham,
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1865

to grant us that we, 
being delivered from the hand of our enemies,
might serve him without fear,
in holiness and righteousness before 
Him all the days of our life.
For the day has dawned upon us from on high
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1866

to give light to those who sit 
in darkness and in the shadow of death,
to guide our feet into the way of peace. 
(Luke 1:68-79)
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1867

The Leader pours a cup of wine 
and recites the following blessing:

(Leader)  Behold, God is my salvation.

(Group)  I will trust and will not be afraid. 
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1868

(Leader)  For God the Lord 
is my strength and song.

(Group)  And he has become my salvation.

1869

draw water from the wells of salvation.
Salvation belongs to the Lord.

(Group)  Your blessing be upon your people.
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1870

(Leader)  The Lord of Hosts is with us.

(Group)  The God of Jacob is our refuge.
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1871

(Leader)  God’s people had light and joy 
and gladness and honor.

(Group)  So be it with us.
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1872

(Leader)  I will lift the cup of salvation and call upon the Name of the Lord.  Blessed are you, Lord our God, King of the Universe, who have created the fruit of the vine.

(Group)  Amen.


The Leader drinks from the cup, passes it, and takes the bread. 
He then recites the following blessing:

CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1873

(Leader)  Blessed are you, Lord our God, 
King of the Universe, who bring forth 
bread from the earth.

(Group)  Amen.

The Leader distributes the bread to the others present 
and begins the meal.

1874

(Leader)  Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the Universe, who sanctify us through faith in the resurrection of Christ and bless us in this day set apart to celebrate His victory.

1875

who have given us new life in your Son.

(Group)  Amen.

The Leader drinks from the cup, passes it, and recites the following prayer while the cup is being passed.

CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1876

(Leader)  Father in Heaven, grant that we may begin the working days which draw near to us in peace; freed from all sin and transgression, filled with the joy of Your Holy Spirit, and following in the ways of Your Son Jesus. 
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1877

May we be fully clothed in him, the New Man, created after your likeness in true righteousness and holiness.  Father of Mercy, bless and prosper the work of our hands. Make us  fruitful in every good work, unweary in well-doing,
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1878

knowing that in Christ our labor is not in vain. May all that we do, in word or deed, be to the praise of your glory through Jesus your Son. Our God and King, guide and defend the people called by Your Name.
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1879

Strengthen and prosper all who cherish thoughts of good towards your people, and fulfill their purposes, but frustrate those who devise evil plans against your people, and make their designs of no effect; as it is said,
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1880

“Take counsel together, 
but it will come to nothing; 
speak a word, but it will not stand, 
for God is with us” (Isaiah 8:10).
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1881

Open to us, Father of Mercies and Lord of Forgiveness, in this week and in the weeks to come, the gates of heavenly help and rejoicing, of holiness and peace, of the study of your teaching and of prayer. 
In us also let the Scripture be fulfilled:
CELEBRATING THE LORD’S DAY
1882

How beautiful on the mountains are the feet of him who brings good news, who heralds peace, brings happiness, proclaims salvation, who says to Zion, “Your God reigns.” (Isaiah 52:7)

(Group)  Amen.

The Leader extinguishes the Lord’s Day candle, if it has been left burning throughout the day. A song may be sung to conclude.

PRAYER FOR EVANGELIZATION

In grateful response to the exceeding goodness of God and the call He has given in the Lord Jesus Christ, I will live as an active member of
Ang Lingkod ng Panginoon,
committing myself to:
 
STATEMENT OF COMMITMENT
1920

1. LIVE FULLY FOR JESUS CHRIST AS HIS DISCIPLE through:
Faithfulness to daily prayer
Regularly reading and meditate on God’s word in Scripture
Living a life of righteousness, free from serious sins
Active participation in the life of the Church
STATEMENT OF COMMITMENT
1921

2. LIVE IN COMMITTED RELATIONSHIPS WITH OTHERS by:
Supporting and living the way of life of Lingkod
Participating in and being faithful to my Action Group and other meetings of Lingkod
Loving the brothers and sisters of Lingkod
Living in right relationships with family members
STATEMENT OF COMMITMENT
1922

3. LIVE A LIFE DEDICATED TO CHRISTIAN SERVICE by:
Giving generously of my time and gifts to serve in daily life and outreach work of Lingkod
Calling others to a full life in Jesus Christ
Building up the members of the body
STATEMENT OF COMMITMENT
1923

4. WORK TOWARDS THE TRANSFORMATION OF MY WORKPLACE AND SOCIETY by:
Putting good order in my personal life and work
Making Christ present in my place of work
Being a good steward of all resources entrusted to me
Standing up against unrighteousness and immorality and relating 
Taking an active concern for the poor and the needy
STATEMENT OF COMMITMENT
1924

May the Lord give me the grace 
to be faithful in living out this commitment everyday for the good of my brothers 
and sisters in Ang Lingkod ng Panginoon 
and for God’s greater glory.
Amen.

HOW GREAT IS OUR GOD

Every tongue confess Him,

AT THE NAME OF JESUS
Verse 1

Humbled for a season
To receive a name
From the lips of sinners
Unto which He came;
Faithfully He bore it spotless to the last,
Brought it back victorious
When through death He passed.


AT THE NAME OF JESUS
Verse 2

Bore it up triumphant with its human light,
Through all ranks of creatures
To the central height,
To the throne of Godhead,
To the Father’s breast,
Filled it with the glory of that perfect rest.

AT THE NAME OF JESUS
Verse 3

In your hearts enthrone Him,
There let Him subdue,
All that is not holy, all that is not true,
Crown Him as your Captain
In temptation’s hour
Let His will enfold you in its light and pow’r.
AT THE NAME OF JESUS
Verse 4

Jesus Lord and Savior shall return again,
With His Father’s Glory o’er the earth to reign.
For all wreaths of Empires 
Meet upon His brow,
And our hearts confess Him,
King of Glory now.
AT THE NAME OF JESUS
Verse 5 (End)

I give glory to the Lamb!

Brief sorrow short-lived care:
The life that knows no ending,
The tearless life is there.

For mortals and for sinners,
A mansion with the blest.

The shadows shall decay.

Shall shine as doth the day.

Praise the Lord, praise His name,
You servants of the Lord.
Praise Him you people of the Lord’s house
In the temple of our God.
PSALM 135
Refrain

Refrain:
Praise the Lord for He is good
Sing praises to Him who is kind.
He who has chosen us Israel
As children of His own.
PSALM 135
Refrain

There shall be no other god,
Nor idols that men have made.
For they who call on their false gods 
Come tumbling to the ground.
PSALM 135
Verse 2

But we shall praise our God,
Praise Him priests of the Lord.
Praise Him all you that worship Him
The King of Zion enthroned.
PSALM 135
Verse 2

Refrain:
Praise the Lord, praise His name,
You servants of the Lord.
Praise Him you people of the Lord’s house
In the temple of our God.
PSALM 135
Refrain

Refrain:
Praise the Lord for He is good
Sing praises to Him who is kind.
He who has chosen us Israel
As children of His own.

Ending:  
Praise the Lord! 
PSALM 135
Refrain (End)

Ngayo’y tuloy pa rin ang ating kilos Kristiyano
Tuloy pa rin ang laban ng kawal ni Kristo.
At kahit na maghirap sa mata ng mundo
Kami nama’y mayaman sa pag-ibig ni Kristo.
KAY KRISTO BUONG BUHAY HABANGBUHAY
Berso 1

Koro:
Kay Kristo buong buhay,
Kay Kristo habangbuhay.
Sigaw natin mula ngayon
At magpakailanman.
KAY KRISTO BUONG BUHAY HABANGBUHAY
Koro

Halina kabataan buhay mo ay pagmasdan
Kanino mo inalay, ito ba ay may saysay?
Halina sa liwanag, halina sa daan
Halina sa Panginoon tangi Siya’ng kaligtasan.
KAY KRISTO BUONG BUHAY HABANGBUHAY
Berso 2

Koro:
Kay Kristo buong buhay,
Kay Kristo habangbuhay.
Sigaw natin mula ngayon
At magpakailanman.
KAY KRISTO BUONG BUHAY HABANGBUHAY
Koro

Huwag mong sabihin ikaw ay 
Bata’t walang magagawa 
Ako’ng naglalagay sa iyong bibig ng salita.
At kahit mayroong takot,
May lakas akong dulot.
Sigaw mo sa buong mundo,
Buhay mo ay kay Kristo.
KAY KRISTO BUONG BUHAY HABANGBUHAY
Berso 3

Koro:
Kay Kristo buong buhay,
Kay Kristo habangbuhay.
Sigaw natin mula ngayon
At magpakailanman.
KAY KRISTO BUONG BUHAY HABANGBUHAY
Koro

Kapag lumubog ang araw atin Siyang makikita
Panandaliang hirap, habangbuhay ang saya.
At kahit nasa langit, ito rin ang sandig
Kay Kristo buong buhay,
Sa Kanya habangbuhay.
KAY KRISTO BUONG BUHAY HABANGBUHAY
Berso 4

Koro:
Kay Kristo buong buhay,
Kay Kristo habangbuhay.
Sigaw natin mula ngayon
At magpakailanman.
KAY KRISTO BUONG BUHAY HABANGBUHAY
Koro

Pangwakas:
Kay Kristo buong buhay,
Kay Kristo habangbuhay.
Mula noon hanggang wakas
Pag-ibig Niya’y wagas.
KAY KRISTO BUONG BUHAY HABANGBUHAY
End

Panginoon, dinggin Mo,
Puso kong nagnanais sa Iyo
Ang tanging alay ko, diwa ko,
Buhay kong mula sa Iyo.
PANGINOON DINGGIN MO
Berso 1

Ang pangitaing inimulat Mo,
Siyang gabay, na susundin ko
Ang pagsubok, iaalay sa ‘Yo,
Mapasa ‘Yo lamang ang buhay ko.
PANGINOON DINGGIN MO
Berso 1

Panginoon, dinggin Mo,
Sala ko’y ipagpatawad Mo
Ang tanging mithi ko, kunin Mo
Lahat ng hadlang patungo sa ‘Yo.
PANGINOON DINGGIN MO
Berso 2

Panginoon ‘kaw ang tanggulan ko,
Siyang patnugot, at pahingahan ko.
Ang lahat, tatalikdan ko, 
Mapasa ‘Yo lamang.
PANGINOON DINGGIN MO
Berso 2

Ang pangitaing inimulat Mo,
Siyang gabay, na susundin ko
Ang pagsubok, iaalay sa ‘Yo, 
Mapasa ‘Yo lamang
Sa ‘Yo lamang ang buhay ko, ang buhay ko.
PANGINOON DINGGIN MO
Berso 2 (End)

He is worthy, He is worthy,
Vict’ry to the Lamb who was slain.
For His blood has won salvation
For every race, kingdom and tribe.
TO THE LAMB
Verse 1

Refrain:
To the Lamb seated on the throne
	Be praise, honor, glory and might.
	He who was, He who is,
	He who shall always be
 
	His name be exalted forever. (3x)
TO THE LAMB
Refrain

He has formed a kingdom on earth
A holy priesthood that serves the Lord.
God’s people will rule the nations,
The reign of Christ will last forever.
TO THE LAMB
Verse 2

Refrain:
To the Lamb seated on the throne
	Be praise, honor, glory and might.
	He who was, He who is,
	He who shall always be
	His name be exalted forever. (3x)

Ending:
His name be exalted forever, Amen.
TO THE LAMB
Refrain (End)

Langit at lupa hinubog Mo
Buhay at tao nilikha Mo
Ngunit anong maiaalay sa ‘Yo
Poon kong mahal?
Kung ‘di ang buhay na mula lamang sa ‘Yo.
PANGINOON SA ‘YO ANG BUHAY KO
Berso 1

Koro:
Panginoon sa ‘Yo (Ikaw) ang buhay ko
Panginoon sa ‘Yo (Ikaw) ang awit ko
Panginoon lahat ng akin ay alay sa ‘Yo
Ay alay sa ‘Yo.
PANGINOON SA ‘YO ANG BUHAY KO
Koro

Langit at lupa hinubog Mo
Lahat ng ito ay pamana Mo.
Ngunit dahil kay Kristo, lahat ng mga bagay
Na maaari kong ibilang na pakinabang
Ay inari kong kalugihan.
PANGINOON SA ‘YO ANG BUHAY KO
Berso 2

Koro:
Panginoon sa ‘Yo (Ikaw) ang buhay ko
Panginoon sa ‘Yo (Ikaw) ang awit ko
Panginoon lahat ng akin ay alay sa ‘Yo
Ay alay sa ‘Yo.
PANGINOON SA ‘YO ANG BUHAY KO
Koro (End)

Who was, who is and is to come.
You are holy, You are holy.

Almighty Lord.
 For You have created everything.
You are worthy, You are worthy,
Worthy Lord.
 
YOU ARE HOLY

Holy, You are holy, holy, Almighty Lord.

Ending:
Holy Lord 
YOU ARE HOLY
Refrain (End)

Hallelujah, Hallelujah!
Your death was not the end.
Alive You reign  in glory now,
Upon Your throne in heav’n.
HALLELUJAH KING OF KINGS
Verse 1

We look to You and give You praise,
With saints and angels voices raise.
You are Lord! Our only Lord!
Victorious King of kings!
HALLELUJAH KING OF KINGS
Verse 1

The battle won, the price for sin
Now paid upon the cross;
Without Your death, the gates of heav’n 
Would be forever closed.

HALLELUJAH KING OF KINGS
Verse 2

Lord Jesus Christ, we give You praise;
In love You chose to take our place.
You are Lord! Our only Lord!
Victorious King of kings!
HALLELUJAH KING OF KINGS
Verse 2

And so we set our gaze 
Upon the glory that awaits.
Before Your throne, we’ll take our place,
And see You face to face.
Our Risen Christ, we give You praise,
In acclamation hands we raise.
You are Lord! Our only Lord!
HALLELUJAH KING OF KINGS
Verse 3

Hallelujah! King of kings!
Hallelujah! King of kings!
Hallelujah! King of kings!
HALLELUJAH KING OF KINGS
Coda (End)

and joy to my heart, a light for my eyes 
and a lamp for my feet
 all of my days
 all of my days

SUNDAY (Week 1)
Psalm 63:2-9
(Leader) As morning breaks I look to you, O God, to be my strength this day, alleluia

(Cantor) O God, you are my God, for you I long;
for you my soul is thirsting.
My body pines for you
like a dry, weary land without water.
So I gaze on you in the sanctuary
to see your strength and your glory.

(All) For your love is better than life,
my lips will speak your praise.
So I will bless you all my life,
in your name I will lift up my hands.
My soul shall be filled as with a banquet,
my mouth shall praise you with joy.

(Cantor) On my bed I remember you.
On you I muse through the night
for you have been my help;
in the shadow of your wings I rejoice.
My soul clings to you;
your right hand holds me fast.  Glory…

(All) As morning breaks I look to you, O God, to be my strength this day, alleluia


Psalm 149
(Leader) Let the people of Zion rejoice in their king, alleluia.

(Cantor) Sing a new song to the Lord,
his praise in the assembly of the faithful.
Let Israel rejoice in its maker,
let Zion’s sons exult in their king.
Let them praise His name with dancing
and make music with timbrel and harp.

(All) For the Lord takes delight in his people.
He crowns the poor with salvation.
Let the faithful rejoice in their glory,
shout for joy and take their rest.
Let the praise of God be in their lips
and a two-edged sword in their hand,

(Cantor) To deal out vengeance to the nations
and punishment to all the peoples;
to bind their kings in chains
and their nobles in fetters of iron;
to carry out the sentence pre-ordained;
this honor is for all His faithful.  Glory…

(All) Let the people of Zion rejoice in their king, alleluia.


Monday (Week 1)
Psalm 5
(Leader) I lift up my heart to you, O Lord, and you will hear my morning prayer.

(Cantor) To my words give ear, O Lord,
give heed to my groaning.
Attend to the sound of my cries,
my King and my God.

(All) It is you whom I invoke, O Lord.
In the morning you hear me;
in the morning I offer you my prayer,
watching and waiting.

(Cantor) You are no God who loves evil;
no sinner is your guest.
The boastful shall not stand their ground
before your face.

(All) You hate all who do evil;
you destroy all who lie.
The deceitful and bloodthirsty man
the detests.

(Cantor) But I through the greatness of your love
have access to your house.
I bow down before your holy temple.
Filled with awe.

+  (All) Lead me, Lord, in your justice,
because of those who lie in wait;
make clear your way before me.

(Cantor) No truth can be found in their mouths,
their heart is all mischief,
their throat a wide-open grave,
all honey their speech.

(All) All those who protect shall be glad
and ring out their joy.
You shelter them; in you they rejoice,
those who love Your name.

(Cantor) It is you who bless the just man, Lord:
you surround Him with favor as with a shield.  Glory…


(All) I lift up my heart to you, O Lord, and you will hear my morning prayer.




Psalm 29
(Leader) Adore the Lord in his holy court

(Cantor) O give the Lord, you sons of God,
give the Lord glory and power;
give the Lord the glory of His name.
Adore the Lord in his holy court.

(All) The Lord’s voice resounding on the waters,
the Lord on the immensity of waters;
the voice of the Lord, full of power,
the voice of the Lord, full of splendor.

(Cantor) The Lord’s voice shattering the cedars,
the Lord shatters the cedars of Lebanon;
he make Lebanon leap like a calf
and Sirion like a your wild-ox.

+  (All) The Lord’s voice flashes flames of fire.
The Lord’s voice shaking the wilderness,
the Lord shakes the wilderness of Kadesh;
the Lord’s voice rending the oak tree
and stripping the forest bare.

(Cantor) The God of Glory thunders.
In His temple they all cry: “Glory!”
The Lord sat enthroned over the flood;
the Lord sits as king for ever.

(All) The Lord will give strength to his people,
the Lord will bless his people with peace.  Glory…


(All) Adore the Lord in his holy court


Tuesday (Week 1)
Psalm 24
(Leader) The man whose deeds are blameless and whose heart is pure will climb the mountain of the Lord.

(Cantor) The Lord’s is the earth and its fullness,
the world and all its peoples.
It is he who set it on the seas;
on the waters He made it firm.

(All) Who shall climb the mountain of the Lord?
Who shall stand in His holy place?
+   The man with clean hands and pure heart,
who desires not worthless things,
who has not sworn so as to deceive his neighbor.

(Cantor) He shall receive blessings from the Lord
and reward from the God who saves him.
Such are the men who seek Him,
seek the face of the God of Jacob.

+   (All) O gates, lift high your heads;
grow higher, ancient doors.
Let Him enter, the king of glory!

+   (Cantor) Who I the king of glory?
The Lord, the mighty, the valiant,
the Lord, the valiant in war.

+   (All) O gates, lift high your heads;
grow higher, ancient doors.
Let Him enter, the king of glory!

+   (Cantor) Who is he, the king of glory?
He, the Lord of armies,
he is the king of glory.  Glory…

(All) The man whose deeds are blameless and whose heart is pure will climb the mountain of the Lord.




Psalm 33
(Leader) The loyal heart must praise the Lord

(Cantor) Ring out your joy to the Lord, O you just;
for praise is fitting for loyal hearts.


(All) Give thanks to the Lord upon the lyre,
with a ten-stringed harp sing Him songs.
O sing Him a song that is new,
play loudly, will all your skill.

(Cantor) For the word of the Lord is faithful
and all His works to be trusted.
The Lord loves justice and right
and fills the earth with His love.

(All) By His word the heavens were made,
by the breath of his mouth all the stars.
He collects the waves of the ocean;
he stores up the depths of the sea.

(Cantor) Let all the earth fear the Lord,
all who live in the world revere Him.
He spoke; and it came to be.
he commanded; it sprang into being.

(All) He frustrates the designs of the nations,
he defeats the plans of the peoples.
His own designs shall stand for ever,
the plans of his heart from age to age.

(Cantor) They are happy, whose God is the Lord,
the people he has chosen as his own.
From the heavens the Lord looks forth,
he sees all the children of men.

(All) From the place where he dwells He gazes
on all the dwellers of the earth,
he who shapes the hearts of them all
and considers all their deeds.

(Cantor) A king is not saved by his army,
nor a warrior preserved by his strength.
A vain hope for safety is the horse;
despite its power it cannot save.

(All) The Lord looks on those who revere him,
on those who hope in his love,
to rescue their souls from death,
to keep them alive in famine.

(Cantor) Our soul is waiting for the Lord.
The Lord is our help and our shield.
In him do our hearts find joy.
We trust in his Holy Name.

(All) May your love be upon us, O Lord,
as we place all our hope in you.  Glory…


(All) The loyal heart must praise the Lord.


Wednesday (Week 1)
Psalm 36
(Leader) Our Lord, in your light we see light itself.

(Cantor) Sin speaks to the sinner
in the depths of his heart.
There is no fear of God
before his eyes

(All) He so flatters himself in hi mind
that he knows not his guilt.
In his mouth are mischief and deceit.
All wisdom is gone.

(Cantor) He plots the defeat of goodness
as he lies on his bed.
He has set his foot on evil ways,
he clings to what is evil.

(All) Your love, Lord, reaches to heaven;
your truth to the skies.
Your justice is like God’s mountain,
your judgments like the deep.

(Cantor) To both man and the beast you give protection.
O Lord, how precious is your love
my God, the sons of men
find refuge in the shelter of your wings.

(All) They feast on the riches of your house;
they drink from the stream of your delight.
In you is the source of life
and in your light we see light.

(Cantor) Keep on loving those who know you,
doing justice for upright hearts.
Let the foot of the proud not crush me
nor the hand of the wicked cast me out.

(All) See how the evil-doers fall!
Flung down, they shall never arise.  Glory…

(All) O Lord, in your light we see light itself.

Psalm 47
(Leader) Exult in God’s presence with hymns of praise.

(Cantor) All peoples, clap your hands,
cry to God with shouts of joy!
For the Lord, the Most  High, we must fear,
great king over all the earth.

(All) He subdues peoples under us
and nations under our feet.
Our inheritance, our glory, is from Him,
give to Jacob out of love.

(Cantor) God goes up with shouts of joy;
the Lord goes up with trumpet blast.
Sing praise for God, sing praise,
sing praise to our King, sing praise.

(All) God is King of all the earth.
Sing praise with all your skill.
God is King over the nations;
God reigns on His holy throne.

(Cantor) The princes of the peoples are assembled
with the people of Abraham’s God.
The rulers of the earth belong to God,
to God who reigns over all.  Glory…

(All) Exult in God’s presence with hymns of praise.

Thursday (Week 1)
Psalm 57
(Leader) Awake, lyre and harp, with praise let us awake the dawn

(Cantor) Have mercy on me, God, have mercy
for in you my soul has taken refuge.
In the shadow of your wings I take refuge
till the storms of destruction pass by.

(All) I call to God the Most High,
to God who has always been my help.
May He send from heaven and save me
and shame those who assail me.

+   (Cantor) May God send His truth and His love.
My soul lies down among lions,
who would devour the sons of men.
Their teeth are spears and arrows,
their tongue a sharpened sword.

(All) O God, arise above the heavens;
may your glory shine one earth!

(Cantor) They laid a snare for my steps,
my soul was bowed down.
They dug a pit in my path
but fell in it themselves.

(All) My heart is ready, O God,
my heart is ready.

(Cantor) I will sing, I will sing your praise.
Awake, my soul,
awake, lyre and harp,
I will awake the dawn.


(All) I will thank you, Lord, among the peoples,
among the nations I will praise you
for your love reaches to the heavens
and your truth to the skies.

(Cantor) O God, arise above the heavens;
may Your glory shine on earth!  Glory…


(All) Awake, lyre and harp, with praise let us awake the dawn.


Psalm 48
(Leader) The Lord is great and worthy to be praised in the city of our God.

(Cantor) The Lord is great and worthy to be praised
in the city of our God.
His holy mountain rises in beauty,
the joy of all the earth.

(All) Mount Zion, true pole of the earth,
the Great King’s city!
God, in the midst of its citadels,
has shown himself its stronghold.

(Cantor) For the kings assembled together,
together they advanced.
They saw; at once they were astounded;
dismayed, they fled in fear.

(All) A trembling seized them there,
like the pangs of birth.
By the east wind you have destroyed
the ships of Tarshish.

(Cantor) As we have heard, so we have seen
in the city of God,
in the city of the Lord of hosts
which God upholds for ever.

(All) O God, we ponder Your love
within Your temple.
Your praise, O God, like your name
reaches to the ends of the earth.

(Cantor) With justice your right hand is filled.
Mount Zion rejoices;
the people of Judah rejoice
at the sight of your judgements.

(All) Walk through Zion, walk all round it;
count the number of its towers.
Review all its ramparts,
examine its castles,

(Cantor) That you may tell the next generation
that such is our God,
our God for ever and always.
It is He who leads us.  Glory…

(All) The Lord is great and worthy to be praised in the city of our God.


Friday (Week 1)
Psalm 51
(Leader) Lord, you will accept the true sacrifice offered on your altar.

(Cantor) Have mercy on me, God, in your kindness.
In your compassion blot out my offense.
O wash me more and more from my guilt
and cleanse me from my sin.

(All) My offense truly I know them;
my sin is always before me.
Against you, you alone, have I sinned;
what is evil in your sight I have done.

(Cantor) That you may be justified when you give sentence
and be without reproach when you judge.
O see, in guilt I was born,
a sinner was conceived.

(All) Indeed you love the truth in the heart;
then in the secret of my heart teach me wisdom.
O purify me, then I shall be clean
o wash me, I shall be whiter than snow.

(Cantor) Make me hear rejoicing and gladness,
that the bones you have crushed may revive.
From my sins turn away your face
and blot out all my guilt.

(All) A pure heart create for me, O God,
put a steadfast spirit within me.
Do not cast me away from your presence,
nor deprive me of your Holy Spirit.

(Cantor) Give me again the joy of your help;
with a spirit of fervor sustain me,
that I may teach transgressors your ways
and sinners may return to you.

(All) O rescue me, God, my helper,
and my tongue shall ring out goodness.
O Lord, open my lips
and my mouth shall declare your praise.

(Cantor) For in sacrifice you take no delight,
burnt offering from me you would refuse,
my sacrifice, a contrite spirit.
A humbled, contrite heart you will not spurn.

(All) In your goodness, show favor to Zion:
rebuild the walls of Jerusalem.
Then you will pleased with lawful sacrifice,
holocausts offered on your altar.  Glory…

(All) Lord, you will accept the true sacrifice offered on your altar.

Psalm 100
(Leader) Let us go into God’s presence singing for joy.

+   (Cantor) Cry out with joy to the Lord, all the earth.
Serve the Lord with gladness.
Come before Him, singing for joy.

+   (All) Know the he, the Lord, is God.
He made us, we belong to him,
We are his people, the sheep of his flock.

+   (Cantor) Go within his gates, giving thanks.
Enter his courts with songs of praise.
Give thanks to him and bless his name.

+   (All) Indeed, how good is the Lord,
Eternal His merciful love.
He is faithful from age to age.  Glory…

(All) Let us go into God’s presence singing for joy.

Saturday (Week 1)
Psalm 119:145-152
(Leader) Dawn finds me ready to welcome you, my God.

(Cantor) I will call with all my heart; Lord, hear me,
I will keep your commands.
I will call upon you, save me
and I will do your will.

(All) I rise before dawn and cry for help;
I hope in your word.
My eyes watch through the night
to ponder your promise.

(Cantor) In Your love hear my voice, O Lord;
give me life by your decrees.
Those who harm me unjustly drew near:
they are afar from your law.

(All) But, you O Lord, are close:
your commands are truth.
Long have I known that your will
is established forever.  Glory…


(All) Dawn finds me ready to welcome you, my God.


Psalm 117
(Leader) Blessed is he who comes in the name of the Lord, alleluia.

(Cantor) O praise the Lord, all you nations,
acclaim Him, all you peoples!

(All) Strong is His love for us;
he is faithful for ever.  Glory…

Sunday (Week 2)
Psalm 118
(Leader) Blessed is he who comes in the name of the Lord, alleluia.

(Cantor) Give thanks to the Lord for he is good,
for his love endures for ever.

(All) Let the sons of Israel say:
“His love endures for ever.”
Let the sons of Aaron say:
“His love endures for ever.”
Let those who fear the Lord say:
“His love endures for ever.”

(Cantor) I called to the Lord in my distress;
he answered and freed me.
The Lord is at my side; I do not fear.
What can man do against me?
The Lord is at my side as my helper;
I shall look down on my foes.

(All) It is better to take refuge in the Lord
than to trust in men:
it is better to take refuge in the Lord
than to trust in princes.

(Cantor) The nations all encompassed me;
in the Lord’s name I crushed them.
They encompassed me, compassed me about;
in the Lord’s name I crushed them.
+   They compassed me about like bees;
they blazed life a fire among thorns.
In the Lord’s name I crushed them.

(All) I was hard-pressed and was falling
but the Lord came to help me.
The Lord is my strength and my song;
he is my savior.
There are shouts of joy and victory
in the tents of the just.

(Cantor) The Lord’s right had has triumphed;
his right hand raised me.
+   The Lord’s right hand has triumphed;
I shall not die, I shall live
and recount His deeds.
I was punished, I was punished by the Lord,
but not doomed to die.

(All) Open to me the gate of holiness:
I will enter and give thanks.
This is the Lord’s own gate.
Where the just may enter.
I will thank you for you have answered
and you are my savior.

(Cantor) The stone which the builders rejected
has become the corner stone.
This is the work of the Lord,
a marvel in our eyes.
This day was made by the Lord;
we rejoice and are glad.

(All) O Lord, grant us salvation;
o Lord, grant us success.
Blessed in the name of the Lord
is he who comes.
We bless you from the house of the Lord;
the Lord is our light.

(Cantor) Go forward in procession with branches
even to the altar.
You are my God, I thank you.
My God, I praise you.
Give thanks to the Lord for He is good;
for His love endures for ever.  Glory…

(All) Blessed is he who comes in the name of the Lord, alleluia.

Psalm 150
(Leader) Praise the Lord for his infinite greatness, alleluia.

(Cantor) Praise God in his holy place,
praise him in his mighty heavens.
Praise him for his powerful deeds,
praise his surpassing greatness.

(All) O praise him with sound of trumpet,
praise him with lute and harp.
Praise him with timbrel and dance,
praise him with strings and pipes.

(Cantor) O praise him with resounding cymbals,
praise him with clashing of cymbals.
Let everything that lives and that breathes
give praise to the Lord.  Glory…

(All) Praise the Lord for his infinite greatness, alleluia.

Monday (Week 2)
Psalm 42
(Leader) When will I come to the end of my pilgrimage and enter the presence of God?

(Cantor) Like the deer that yearns
for running streams,
so my soul is yearning
for you, my God

(All) My soul is thirsting for God,
the God of my life;
when can I enter and see
the face of God?

(Cantor) My tears have become my bread,
by night, by day,
as I hear it said all the day long:
“Where is your God?”

(All) These things will I remember
as I pour out my soul:
how I would lead the rejoicing crowd
into the house of God,
amid cries of gladness and thanksgiving,
the throng wild with joy.

(Cantor) Why are you cast down, my soul,
why groan within me?
Hope in God; I will praise Him still,
my savior and my God.

(All) My soul is cast down within me
as I think of you
from the country of Jordan and Mount Hermon,
from the Hill of Mizar.

(Cantor) Deep is calling on deep,
in the roar of waters:
your torrents and all your waves
swept over me.

(All) By day the Lord will send
his loving kindness;
by night I will sing to him,
praise the God of my life.

(Cantor) I will say to God, my rock:
“Why have you forgotten me?
Why do I go mourning,
oppressed by the foe?”


(All) With cries that pierce me to the heart,
my enemies revile me,
aaying to me all the day long:
“Where is your God?”

(Cantor) Why are you cast down, my soul,
why groan within me?
Hope in God; I will praise him still,
my savior and my God.  Glory…

(All) When will I come to the end of my pilgrimage and enter the presence of God?


Psalm 19a
(Leader) The vaults of heaven ring with your praise, O Lord.

(Cantor) The heavens proclaim the glory of God
and the firmament shows forth the work of his hands.
Day unto day takes up the story
and night unto night makes known the message.

+  (All) No speech, no word, no voice is heard
yet their span extends through all the earth,
their words to the utmost bounds of the world.

+  (Cantor) There he has placed a tent for the sun,
it comes forth like a bridegroom coming from his tent,
rejoices like a champion to run its course.

+  (All) At the end of the sky is the rising of the sun;
to the furthest end of the sky is its course.
There is nothing concealed from its burning heat.
Glory…

(All) The vaults of heaven ring with your praise, O Lord.


Tuesday (Week 2)
Psalm 43
(Leader) Lord, send forth your light and your truth.

(Cantor) Defend me, O God, and plead my cause
against a godless nation.
From a deceitful and cunning men
rescue me, O God.

(All) Since you, O God, are my stronghold,
why have you rejected me?
Why do I go mourning
oppressed by the foe?

(Cantor) O send forth your light and your truth;
let these be my guide.
Let them bring me to your holy mountain
to the place where you dwell.

(All) And I will come to the altar of God,
the God of my joy.
My redeemer, I will thank you on the harp,
o God, my God.

(Cantor) Why are you cast down, my soul,
why groan within me?
Hope in God; I will praise him still,
my savior and my God.  Glory…

(All) Lord, send forth your light and your truth.

Psalm 65
(Leader) To you, O God, our praise is due in Zion.

(Cantor) To you, our praise is due
in Zion, O God.
To You we pay our vows,
you who hear our prayer.

(All) To you all flesh will come
with its burden of sin.
Too heavy for us our offenses,
but you wipe them away.

(Cantor) Blessed is he whom you choose and call
to dwell in your courts.
We are filled with the blessings of your house,
of your holy temple.

(All) You keep your pledge with wonders,
O God our savior,
the hope of all the earth
and of far distant isles.

(Cantor) You uphold the mountains with your strength,
you are girded with power.
+  You still the roaring of the seas,
the roaring of their waves
and the tumult of the peoples.

(All) The ends of the earth stand in awe
at  the sight of your wonders.
The lands of sunrise and sunset
you fill with your joy.

(Cantor) You care for the earth, give it water,
you fill it with riches.
Your river in heaven brims over
to provide its grain.

(All) And thus you provide for the earth;
you drench its furrows,
you level it, soften it with showers,
you bless its growth.

+  (Cantor) You crown the year with your goodness.
Abundance flows in your steps,
In the pastures of the wilderness it flows.

(All) The hills are girded with joy,
the meadows covered with flocks,
the valleys are decked with wheat.
They shout for joy, yes, they sing.  Glory…

(All) To you, O God, our praise is due in Zion.

Wednesday (Week 2)
Psalm 77
(Leader) O God, all your ways are holy; what god can compare with our God?

+  (Cantor) In the day of my distress I sought the Lord.
My hands were raised at night without ceasing;
my soul refused to be consoled.
I remembered my God and I groaned.
I pondered and my spirit fainted.


(All) You withheld sleep from my eyes.
I was troubled, I could not speak.
I thought of the days of long ago
and remembered the years long past.
At night I mused within my heart.
I pondered and my spirit questioned.

(Cantor) “Will the Lord reject us forever?
Will He show us His favor no more?
Has His love vanished for ever?
Has His promise come to an end?
Does God forget His mercy
or in anger withhold His compassion?”


(All) I said: “This is what causes my grief;
that the way of the Most High has changed.”
I remember the deeds of the Lord,
I remember your wonders of old,
I muse on all your works
and ponder your mighty deeds.

(Cantor) Your ways, O God, are holy.
What god is great as our God?
You are the God who works wonders.
You showed your power among the peoples.
Your strong arm redeemed your people,
The sons of Jacob and Joseph.



(All) The waters saw you, O God,
the waters saw you and trembled;
the depths were moved with terror.
The clouds poured down rain,
the skies sent forth their voice;
your arrows flashed to and fro.

(Cantor) Your thunder rolled round the sky,
your flashes lighted up the world.
The earth was moved and trembled
when your way led through the sea,
your path through the mighty waters,
and no one saw your footprints.



(All) You guided your people like a flock
by the hand of Moses and Aaron.  Glory…

(All) O God, all your ways are holy; what god can compare with our God?

Psalm 97
(Leader) The Lord is king, let the earth rejoice.

(Cantor) The Lord is king, let the earth rejoice,
let all the coastlands be glad.
Cloud and darkness are his raiment;
his throne, justice and right.


(All) A fire prepares His path;
it burns up His foes on every side.
His lightnings light up the world,
the earth trembles at the sight.

(Cantor) The mountains melt like wax
before the Lord of all the earth.
The skies proclaim His justice;
all peoples see his glory.


+  (All) Let those who serve idols be ashamed,
those who boast of their worthless gods.
All your spirits, worship him.

+  (Cantor) Zion hears and is glad;
the people of Judah rejoice
because of your judgements, O Lord.


+  (All) For you indeed are the Lord,
most high above all the earth,
exalted far above all spirits.

+  (Cantor) The Lord loves those who hate evil;
he guards the souls of his saints;
he sets them free from the wicked.


(All) Light shines forth for the just
and joy for the upright in heart.
Rejoice, you just, in the Lord;
give glory to his holy name.  Glory…

(All) The Lord is king, let the earth rejoice.

Thursday (Week 2)
Psalm 80
(Leader) Stir up your mighty power, Lord; come to our aid.

(Cantor) O shepherd of Israel, hear us,
you who lead Joseph’s flock,
shine forth from your cherubim throne
upon Ephraim, Benjamin, Manasseh.
O Lord, rouse up your might,
o Lord, come to our help.

(All) God of hosts, bring us back;
let your face shine on us and we shall be saved.

(Cantor) Lord God of hosts, how long
will you frown on your people’s plea?
You have fed them with tears for their bread,
an abundance of tears for their drink.
You have made us the taunt of our neighbors,
our enemies laugh us to scorn.

(All) God of hosts, bring us back;
let your face shine on us and we shall be saved.

(Cantor) You brought a vine out of Egypt;
to plant it you drove out the nations.
Before it you cleared the ground;
it took root and spread through the land

(All) The mountains were covered with its shadow,
the cedars of God with its boughs.
It stretched out its branches to the sea,
to the Great River it stretched out its shoots.

(Cantor) Then why have you broken down its walls?
It is plucked by all who pass by.
It is ravaged by the boar of the forest,
devoured by the beasts of the field.

(All) God of hosts, turn again, we implore,
look down from heaven and see.
Visit this vine and protect it,
the vine your right hand has planted.
Men have burnt it with fire and destroyed it.
May they perish at the frown of your face.

(Cantor) May your hand be on the man you have chosen,
the man you have given your strength.
And we shall never forsake you again:
give us life that we may call upon your name.


(All) God of hosts, bring us back;
Let your face shine on us and we shall be saved.  Glory…

(All) Stir up your mighty power, Lord: come to our aid.

Friday (Week 2)
Psalm 51
(Leader) A humble, contrite heart, O God, you will not spurn.

(Cantor) Have mercy on me, O God, in your kindness.
In your compassion blot out my offense.
O wash me more and more from my guilt
and cleanse me from my sin.

(All) My offenses truly I know them;
my sin is always before me.
Against you, you alone, have I sinned;
what is evil in your sight I have done.

(Cantor) That you may be justified when you give sentence
and be without reproach when you judge.
O see, in guilt I was born,
a sinner was I conceived.


(All) Indeed you love truth in the heart;
then in the secret of my heart teach me wisdom.
O purify me, then I shall be clean;
O wash me, I shall be whiter than snow.

(Cantor) Make me hear rejoicing and gladness,
that the bones you have crushed may revive.
From my sins turn away your face
and blot out all my guilt.

(All) A pure heart create for me, O God,
put a steadfast spirit within me.
Do not cast me away from your presence,
nor deprive me of your holy spirit.

(Cantor) Give me again the joy of your help;
with a spirit of fervor sustain me.
That I may teach transgressors your ways
and sinners may return to you.


(All) O rescue me, God, my helper,
and my tongue shall ring out your goodness.
O Lord, open my lips
and my mouth shall declare your praise.

(Cantor) For in sacrifice you take no delight,
burnt offering from me you would refuse,
my sacrifice, a contrite spirit.
A humbled, contrite heart you will not spurn.

(All) In your goodness, show favor to Zion:
rebuild the walls of Jerusalem.
The you will be pleased with lawful sacrifice,
holocausts offered on your altar.  Glory…

(All) A humble, contrite heart, O God, you will not spurn.


Psalm 147:12-20
(Leader) O praise the Lord, Jerusalem!

(Cantor) O praise the Lord Jerusalem
Zion, praise your God!


(All) He has strengthened the bars of your gates,
he has blessed the children within you.
He  established peace on your borders,
he feeds you with finest wheat.

(Cantor) He sends his word to the earth
and swiftly runs his command.
He showers down snow white as wool,
he scatters hoar-frost like ashes.


(All) He hurls down hailstones like crumbs.
The waters are frozen at his touch;
he sends forth his word and it melts them:
at the breath of his mouth the waters flow.

(Cantor) He makes his word known to Jacob,
to Israel his laws and decrees.
He has not dealt thus with other nations;
he has not taught them his decrees.  Glory…

(All) O praise the Lord, Jerusalem!


Saturday (Week 2)
Psalm 92
(Leader) As morning breaks we sing of your mercy, Lord, and night will find us proclaiming your fidelity.

(Cantor) It is good to give thanks to the Lord,
to make music to your name, O Most High,
to proclaim your love in the morning
and your truth in the watches of the night,
on the ten-stringed lyre and the lute,
with the murmuring sound of the harp.

(All) Your deeds, O Lord, have made me glad;
for the work of your hands I shout with joy.
O Lord, how great are your works!
How deep are your designs!
The foolish man cannot know this
and the fool cannot understand.

(Cantor) Though the wicked spring up like grass
and all who do evil thrive:
they are doomed to be eternally destroyed.
But you, Lord, are eternally on high.
See how your enemies perish;
all doers of evil are scattered.

(All) To me you give the wild-ox’s strength;
you anoint me with the purest oil.
My eyes looked in triumph on my foes;
my ears heard gladly of their fall.
The just will flourish like the palm-tree
and grow like a Lebanon cedar.

(Cantor) Planted in the house of the Lord
they will flourish in the courts of our God,
still bearing fruit when the are old,
still full of sap, still green,
to proclaim that the Lord is just;
in him, my rock, there is no wrong.  Glory…

(All) As morning breaks we sing of your mercy, Lord, and night will find us proclaiming your fidelity.


Psalm 8
(Leader) How wonderful is your name, O Lord, in all creation.

(Cantor) How great is your name, O Lord our God,
through all the earth!

(All) Your majesty is praised above the heavens;
on the lips of children and of babes
you have found praise to foil your enemy,
to silence the foe and the rebel.

(Cantor) When I see the heavens, the work of your hands,
the moon and the stars which you arranged,
what is man that you should keep him in mind,
mortal man that you care for him?

(All) Yet you have made him little less than a god;
with glory and honor you crowned him,
gave him power over the works of your hand,
put all things under his feet.

(Cantor) All of them, sheep and cattle,
yes, even the savage beasts,
birds of the air, and fish
that make their way through the waters.

(All) How great is your name, O Lord, our God,
through all the earth!  Glory…

(All) How wonderful is your name, O Lord, in all creation.

Sunday (Week 3)
Psalm 93
(Leader) Glorious is the Lord on high, alleluia.

+  (Cantor) The Lord is king, with majesty enrobed;
the Lord has robed himself with might,
he has girded himself with power.

 +  (All) The world you made firm, not to be moved;
your throne has stood firm from the old.
From all eternity, O Lord, you are.

+  (Cantor) The waters have lifted up, O Lord,
the waters have lifted up their voice,
the waters have lifted up their thunder.

+  (All) Greater than the roar of mighty waters,
more glorious than the surgings of the sea,
the Lord is glorious on high.

+  (Cantor) Truly your decrees are to be trusted.
Holiness is fitting to your house,
O Lord, until the end of time.  Glory…

(All) Glorious is the Lord on high, a lleluia.

Psalm 148
(Leader) Praise the Lord from the heavens, alleluia.

(Cantor) Praise the Lord from the heavens,
praise him in the heights.
Praise him, all his angels,
praise him, all his host.


(All) Praise him, sun and moon,
praise him, shining stars,
praise him, highest heavens,
and the waters above the heavens.

(Cantor) Let them praise the name of the Lord.
He commanded: they were made.
He fixed them for ever,
gave a law which shall not pass away.

(All) Praise the Lord from the earth,
sea creatures and all oceans,
fire and hail, snow and mist,
stormy winds that obey his word;

(Cantor) all mountains and hills,
all fruit trees and cedars,
beasts, wild and tame,
reptile and birds on the wing;

(All) all earth’s kings and peoples,
earth’s princes and rulers;
young men and maidens,
old men together with children.

(Cantor) Let them praise the name of the Lord
for he alone is exalted.
The splendor of his name
reaches beyond heaven and earth.

(All) He exalts the strength of his people.
He is the praise of all his saints,
of the sons of Israel,
of the people to whom he comes close.  Glory…

(All) Praise the Lord from the heavens, alleluia.

Monday (Week 3)
Psalm 84
(Leader) Blessed are the who dwell in your house, O Lord.

(Cantor) How lovely is your dwelling place,
Lord, God of hosts.


(All) My soul is longing and yearning,
is yearning for the courts of the Lord.
My heart and my soul ring out their joy
to God, the living God.

(Cantor) The sparrow herself finds a home
and the swallow a nest for her brood;
she lays her young by your altars,
Lord of hosts, my king and my God.


(All) They are happy ,who dwell in your house,
for ever singing your praise.
They are happy, whose strength is in you,
in whose hearts are the roads to Zion.

+  (Cantor) As they go through the Bitter Valley
they make it a place of springs,
the autumn rain covers it with blessings.
They walk with ever growing strength,
they will see the God of gods in Zion.


(All) The Lord God of hosts, hear my prayer,
give ear, O God of Jacob.
Turn your eyes, O God, our shield,
look on the face of your anointed.

(Cantor) One day within your courts
is better than a thousand elsewhere.
The threshold of the house of God
I prefer to the dwellings of the wicked.


(All) For the Lord God is a rampart, a shield;
he will give us his favor and glory.
The Lord will not refuse any good
to those who walk without blame.

(Cantor) Lord, God of hosts,
happy the man who trusts in you!  Glory…

(All) Blessed are they who dwell in your house, O Lord.


Psalm 96
(Leader) Sing to the Lord and bless his name.

+  (Cantor) O sing a new song to the Lord
sing to the Lord, all the earth.
O sing to the Lord, bless his name.


+  (All) Proclaim his help day by day,
tell among the nations his glory,
and his wonders among all the peoples.

+  (Cantor) The Lord is great and worthy of praise,
to be feared above all gods;
the gods of the heathens are naught.


+  (All) It was the Lord who made the heavens,
his are majesty and state and power
and splendor in his holy place.

+  (Cantor) Give the Lord, you families of peoples,
give the Lord glory and power,
give the Lord the glory of his name.


+  (All) Bring an offering and enter his courts,
worship the Lord in his temple.
O earth, tremble before him.

+ (Cantor) Proclaim to the nation: “God is king,”
the world he made firm in its place;
he will judge the peoples in fairness.


(All) Let the heavens rejoice and earth be glad,
let the sea and all within it thunder praise,
let the land and all it bears rejoice,
all the trees of the wood shout for joy

(Cantor) at the presence of the Lord for he comes,
he comes to rule the earth.
With justice he will rule the world,
he will judge the peoples with his truth.  Glory…

(All) Sing to the Lord and bless his name.



Tuesday (Week 3)
Psalm 85
(Leader) Lord, you have blessed your land; you have forgiven the sins of your people.

(Cantor) O Lord, you once favored your land
and revived the fortunes of Jacob,
you forgave the guilt of your people
and covered all their sins.
You averted all your rage,
you calmed the heat of your anger.


(All) Revive us now, God, our helper!
Put an end to your grievance against us.
Will you be angry with us forever,
will your anger never cease?

(Cantor) Will you not restore again our life
that your people may rejoice in you?
Let us see, O Lord, your mercy
and give us your saving help.

(All) I will hear what the Lord God has to say,
a voice that speaks of peace,
peace for his people and his friends
and those who turn to him in their hearts.
His help is near for those who fear him
and his glory will dwell in our land.

(Cantor) Mercy and faithfulness have met;
justice and peace have embraced.
Faithfulness shall spring from the earth
and justice shall look down from heaven.

(All) The Lord will make us prosper
and our earth shall yield its fruit.
Justice shall march before him
and peace shall follow his steps.  Glory…

(All) Lord, you have blessed your land; you have forgiven the sins of your people.


Psalm 67
(Leader) Lord, let the light of your face shine upon us.

(Cantor) O God, be gracious and bless us
and let your face shed its light upon us.
So will your ways be known upon earth
and all nations learn your saving help.

(All) Let the people praise you, O God;
let all the peoples praise you.

(Cantor) Let the nations be glad and exult
for you rule the world with justice.
With fairness you rule the peoples,
you guide the nations on earth.

(All) Let the people praise you, O God;
let all the peoples praise you.

(Cantor) The earth has yielded its fruit
for God, our God, has blessed us.
May God still give us his blessing
till the ends of the earth revere him.

(All) Let the people praise you, O God;
let all the peoples praise you.  Glory…

(All) Lord, let the light of your face shine upon us.

Wednesday (Week 3)
Psalm 86
(Leader) Give joy to your servant, Lord; to you I lift up my heart.

(Cantor) Turn your ear, O Lord, and give answer
for I am poor and needy.
Preserve my life, for I am faithful:
save the servant who trusts in you.

(All) You are my God, have mercy on me, Lord,
for I cry to you all day long.
Give joy to your servant, O Lord,
for to you I lift up my soul.

(Cantor) O Lord, you are good and forgiving,
full of love to all who call.
Give heed, O Lord, to my prayer
and attend to the sound of my voice.

(All) In the day of distress I will call
and surely you  will reply.
Among the gods there is none like you, O Lord;
nor work to compare with yours.

(Cantor) All the nations shall come to adore you
and glorify your name, O Lord:
for you are great and do marvelous deeds,
you who alone are God.

+  (All) Show me, Lord, your way
so that I may walk in your truth.
Guide my hear to fear your name.

(Cantor) I will praise you, Lord my God, with all my hear
and glorify your name for ever;
for your love to me has been great:
you have saved me from the depths of the grave.

+  (All) The proud have risen against me;
ruthless men seek my life:
to you the pay ne heed.

(Cantor) But you, God of mercy and compassion,
slow to anger, O Lord,
abounding in love and truth
turn and take pity on me.



(All) O give your strength to your servant
and save your handmaid’s son.
+ Show me a sign of your favor
that my foes may see to their shame
that you console me and give me your help.  Glory…

(All) Give joy to your servant, Lord; to you I lift up my heart.

Psalm 98
(Leader) Let us celebrate with joy in the presence of our Lord and King.

(Cantor) Sing a new song to the Lord
for he has worked wonders.
His right hand and his holy arm
have brought salvation.

(All) The Lord has made known his salvation;
has shown his justice to the nations.
He has remembered his truth and love
for the house of Israel.

(Cantor) All the ends of the earth have seen
the salvation of our God.
Shout to the Lord, all the earth,
ring out your joy.

(All) Sing psalms to the Lord with the harp
with the sound of music.
With trumpets and the sound of the horn
acclaim the King, the Lord.

(Cantor) Let the sea and all within it thunder;
the world, and all its peoples.
Let the rivers clap their hands
and the hills ring out their joy.


(All) Rejoice at the presence of the Lord,
for he comes to rule the earth.
He will rule the world with justice
and the peoples with fairness.  Glory…

(All) Let us celebrate with joy in the presence of our Lord and King.


Thursday (Week 3)
Psalm 87
(Leader) Glorious things are said of you,  city of God.

(Cantor) On the holy mountain is his city
cherished by the Lord.
The Lord prefers that gates of Zion
to all Jacob’s dwellings.
Of you are told glorious things,
O city of God!


(All) “Babylon and Egypt I will count
among those who know me;
Philistia, Tyre, Ethiopia,
these will be her children
and Zion shall be called ‘Mother’
for all shall be her children.”

(Cantor) It is he, the Lord Most High,
who gives each his place.
In his register of peoples he writes:
“These are her children,” 
and while they dance they will sing:
“In you all find their home.”  Glory…

(All) Glorious things are said of you, O city of God.



Psalm 99
(Leader) Give praise to the Lord our God, bow down before his holy mountain.

+  (Cantor) The Lord is king; the peoples tremble.
The is throned on the cherubim; the earth quakes.
The Lord is great in Zion.

+  (All) He is supreme over all the peoples.
Let them praise his name, so terrible and great.
He is holy, full of power.

+  (Cantor) You are king who loves what is right;
you have established equity, justice and right;
you have established them in Jacob.

+  (All) Exalt the Lord our God;
bow down before Zion, his footstool.
He the Lord is holy.

+  (Cantor) Among his priests were Aaron and Moses,
among those who invoked his name was Samuel.
They invoked the Lord and he answered.

+  (All) To them he spoke on the pillar of cloud.
They did his will; they kept the law,
which he, the Lord, had given.

+  (Cantor) O Lord our God, you answered them.
For them you were a God who forgives;
yet you punish all their offenses.

+  (All) Exalt the Lord our God;
bow down before his holy mountain
for the Lord our God is holy.  Glory…

(All) Give praise to the Lord our God, bow down before his holy mountain.

Friday (Week 3)
Psalm 51
(Leader) You alone I have grieved by my sin; have pity on me, O Lord.

(Cantor) Have mercy on me, God, in your kindness.
In your compassion blot out my offense.
O wash me more and more from my guilt
and cleanse me from my sin.

(All) My offenses truly I know them;
my sin is always before me.
Against you, you alone, have I sinned;
what is evil in your sight I have done.

(Cantor) That you may be justified when you give sentence
and be without reproach when you judge.
O see, in guilt I was born,
a sinner  was I conceived.


(All) Indeed you love truth in the heart;
then in the secret of my heart teach me wisdom.
O purify me, then I shall be clean;
O wash me, I shall be whiter than snow.

(Cantor) Make me hear rejoicing and gladness,
that the bones you have crushed may revive.
From my sins turn away your face
and blot out all my guilt.


(All) A pure heart create for me, O God,
put a steadfast spirit within me.
Do not cast me away from your presence,
nor  deprive me of your holy spirit.

(Cantor) Give me again the joy of your help;
with a spirit of fervor sustain me,
that I may teach transgressors your ways 
and sinners may return to you.


(All) O rescue me, God, my helper,
and  my tongue shall ring out your goodness.
O Lord, open my lips
and  my mouth shall declare your praise.

(Cantor) For in sacrifice you take no delight,
burnt offering from me you would refuse,
my sacrifice, a contrite spirit.
A humbled, contrite heart you will not spurn.


(All) In your goodness, show favor to Zion:
rebuild  the walls of Jerusalem.
Then you will be pleased with lawful sacrifice,
holocausts offered  on your altar.  Glory…

(All) You alone I have grieved by my sin; have pity on me, O Lord.

Psalm 100
(Leader) The Lord is God; we are his people, the flock he shepherds.

+  (Cantor) Cry out with joy to the Lord, all the earth.
Serve the Lord with gladness.
Come before him, singing for joy.

+  (All) Know that he, the Lord, is God.
He made us, we belong to him, 
we are his people, the sheep of his flock.

+  (Cantor) Go within his gates, giving thanks.
Enter his courts with songs of praise.
Give thanks to him and bless his name.

+  (All) Indeed, how good is the Lord,
eternal  his merciful love.
He is faithful from age to age.  Glory…

(All) The Lord is God; we are his people, the flock he shepherds.

Saturday (Week 3)
Psalm 119:145 – 152
(Leader) Lord, you are near to us, and all your ways are true.

(Cantor) I call with all my heart; Lord, hear me,
I will keep your commands.
I call upon you, save me
and I will do your will.

(All) I rise before the dawn and cry for help,
I hope in your word.
My eyes watch through the night
to ponder your promise.

(Cantor) In your love hear my voice, o Lord;
give me life by your decrees.
Those who harm me unjustly drew near
they are far from your law.

(All) But, you O Lord, are close:
your commands are truth.
Long have I known that your will’
is established for ever.  Glory…

(All) Lord, you are near to us, and all your ways are true.


Psalm 117
(Leader) The Lord remains faithful to his promise for ever.

(Cantor) O praise the Lord, all you nations,
acclaim him all you peoples!

(All) Strong is his love for us;
he is faithful for ever.  Glory…

(All) The Lord remains faithful to his promise for ever.


Sunday (Week 4)
Psalm 118
(Leader) Praise the Lord, for his loving kindness will never fail, alleluia.

(Cantor) Give thanks to the Lord for he is good,
for his love endures forever.


(All) Let the sons of Israel say:
“His love endures for ever.”
Let the sons of  Aaron say:
“His love endures for ever.”
Let those who fear the Lord say:
“His love endures for ever.”

(Cantor) I called to the Lord in my distress;
he answered and freed me.
The Lord is at my side; I do not fear.
What can man do against me?
The Lord is at my side as my helper:
I shall look down on my foes.

(All) It is better to take refuge in the Lord
that to trust in men;
it is better to take refuge on the Lord
than to trust in princes.

(Cantor) The nations all encompassed me;
in the Lord’s name I crushed them.
They compassed me, compassed me about;
in the Lord’s name I crushed them.
+ They compassed me about like bees;
they blazed like a fire among thorns.
In the Lord’s name I crushed them. 

(All) I was hard-pressed and was falling,
The but  the Lord came to help me.
The Lord is my strength and my song;
he  is my savior.
There are shouts of joy and victory
in the tents of the just.

(Cantor) The Lord’s right hand has triumphed;
His right hand raised me.
+ The Lord’s right hand has triumphed;
I shall not die, I shall live and recount his deeds.
I was punished, I was punished by the Lord,
but not doomed to die.

(All) Open to me the gates of holiness:
I will enter and give thanks.
This is the Lord’s own gate
where  the just may enter.
I will thank you for you have answered
and  you are my savior.

(Cantor) The stone which the builders rejected
has become the corner stone.
This is the work of the Lord,
a marvel in our eyes.
This day was made by the Lord;
We rejoice and are glad.

(All) O Lord, grant us salvation;
O Lord, grant success.
Blessed in the name of the Lord
is he who comes.
We bless you from the house of the Lord;
the  Lord God is our light.

(Cantor) Go forward in procession with branches
even to the altar.
You are my God, I thank you.
My God, I praise you.
Give thanks to the Lord for he is good;
for his love endures for ever.  Glory…

(All) Praise the Lord, for his loving kindness will never fail, alleluia.



Psalm 150
(Leader) Praise the Lord for his infinite greatness, alleluia.

(Cantor) Praise God in his holy place,
praise him in his mighty heavens.
Praise him for his powerful deeds,
praise his surpassing greatness.

(All) O praise him with sound of trumpet,
praise him with lute and harp.
Praise him with timbrel and dance,
praise him with strings and pipes.

(Cantor) O praise him with resounding cymbals,
praise him with clashing of cymbals.
Let everything that lives and that breathes
give praise to the Lord.  Glory…

(All) Praise the Lord for his infinite greatness, alleluia.

Monday (Week 4)
Psalm 90
(Leader) Each morning, Lord, you fill is with your kindness.

(Cantor) O Lord, you have been our refuge
from one generation to the next.
+  Before the mountains were born
or the earth or the world brought forth,
you are God, without beginning or end.

(All) You turn men back to dust
and say: “Go back, sons of men.”
+ To your eyes a thousand years
are like yesterday, come and gone,
no more than a watch in the night.

(Cantor) You sweep men away like a dream,
like the grass which springs up in the morning.
In the morning it springs up and flowers:
by evening it withers and fades.

(All) So we are destroyed in your anger,
struck with terror in your furry.
Our guilt lies open before you;
our secrets in the light of your face.

(Cantor) All our days pass way in your anger.
Our life is over like a sigh.
Our span is seventy years,
or eighty for those who are strong.

(All) And most of these are emptiness and pain.
They pass swiftly and we are gone.
Who understands the power of your anger
and fears the strength of your fury?

(Cantor) Make us know the shortness of our life
that we may gain wisdom of heart.
Lord, relent! Is your anger for ever?
Show pity to your servants.

(All) In the morning, fill us with your love;
we shall exult and rejoice all our days.
Give us joy to balance our affliction
for the years when we knew misfortune.

(Cantor) Show forth your work to your servants;
let your glory shine on their children.
+  Let the favor of the Lord be upon us:
give success to the work of our hands.
Give success to the work of our hands.  Glory…

(All) Each morning, Lord, you fill us with your kindness.

Psalm 135:1-12
(Leader) You who stand in his sanctuary, praise the name of the Lord.

(Cantor) Praise the name of the Lord,
praise him, servants of the Lord,
who stand in the house of the Lord
in the courts of the house of our God.

(All) Praise the Lord for the Lord is good.
Sing a psalm to his name for the is loving.
For the Lord has chosen Jacob for himself
and Israel for his own possession.

(Cantor) For I know that the Lord is great,
that our Lord is high above all gods.
The Lord does whatever he wills,
in heaven, on earth, in the seas.

+  (All) He summons clouds from the ends of the earth;
makes lightning produce rain;
from his treasuries he sends forth the wind.

(Cantor) The first-born of the Egyptians he smote,
of man and beast alike.
+  Signs and wonders he worked
In the midst of your land, O Egypt,
against Pharaoh and all his servants

(All) Nations in their greatness he struck
and kings in their splendor he slew.
+  Sihon, king of Amorites.
Og, the king of Bashan,
and all the kingdoms of Canaan.
He let Israel inherit their land;
on his people their land he bestowed.  Glory…

(All) You who stand in his sanctuary, praise the name of the Lord.


Tuesday (Week 4)
Psalm 101
(Leader) I will sing to you, O Lord; I will learn from you the way of perfection.

(Cantor) My song is of mercy and justice;
I sing to you, O Lord.
I will walk in the way of perfection.
O when, Lord, will you come?

(All) I will walk with blameless heart
within my house;
I will not set before my eyes
whatever is base.

(Cantor) I will hate the ways of the crooked;
they shall not be my friends.
The false-hearted must keep far away;
the wicked I disown.

(All) The man who slanders his neighbor in secret
I will bring to silence.
The man of proud looks and haughty heart
I will never endure.

(Cantor) I look to the faithful in the land
that they may dwell with me.
He who walks in the way of perfection
shall be my friend.


(All) No man who practices deceit
shall live within my house.
No man who utters lies shall stand
before my eyes.

(Cantor) Morning by morning I will silence
all the wicked in the land,
uprooting from the city of the Lord
all who do evil.  Glory…

(All) I will sing to you, O Lord;
I will learn from you the way of perfection.


Psalm 144:1 – 10
(Leader) O God, I will sing to you a new song.

+  (Cantor) Blessed be the Lord, my rock
Who trains my arms for battle,
Who prepares my hands for war.


(All) He is my love, my fortress;
he is my stronghold, my savior,
my shield, my place of refuge.
He brings people under my rule.

(Cantor) Lord, what is man that you care for him,
mortal man, that you keep him in mind;
man, who is merely a breath,
whose life fades like a passing shadow?

(All) Lower your heavens and come down;
touch the mountains; wreathe them in smoke.
Flash your lightnings, rout the foe,
shoot your arrows and put them to flight.

(Cantor) Reach down from heaven and save me;
draw me out from the mighty waters,
+  from the hands of alien foes
whose mouth are filled with lies,
whose hands are raised in perjury.

(All) To you, O God, will I sing a new song;
I will play on the ten-stringed harp
to you who give kings their victory,
who set David your servant free.  Glory…

(All) O God, I will sing to you a new song.

Wednesday (Week 4)
Psalm 108
(Leader) My heart is ready, O God, my heart is ready.

(Cantor) My heart is ready, O God;
I will sing, sing your praise.
+  Awake, my soul;
awake, lyre and harp,
I will awake the dawn


(All) I will thank you, Lord, among the peoples,
among the nations I will praise you,
for your love reaches to the heavens
and your truth to the skies.
O God, arise above the heavens;
may your glory shine on earth!

(Cantor) O come and deliver your friends;
help with your right hand and reply.
+  From his holy place God has made this promise:
“I will triumph and divide the land of Shechem;
I will measure out the valley of Succoth.


+  (All) Gilead is mine and Manasseh.
Ephraim I take for my helmet,
Judah for my commander’s staff.
+  Moab I will use for my washbowl,
On Edom I will plant my shoe.
Over the Philistines I will shout in triumph.”

(Cantor) But who will lead me to conquer the fortress?
Who will bring me face to face the Edom?
Will you utterly reject us, O God,
And no longer march with our enemies?




(All) Give us help against the foe:
For the help of man is vain.
With God we shall do bravely
And he will trample down our foes.  Glory…

(All) My heart is ready, O God, my heart is ready.

Psalm 146
(Leader) I will praise my God all the days of my life.

+  (Cantor) My soul, give praise to the Lord;
I will praise the Lord all my days,
make music to my God while I live.

(All) Put no trust in princes,
in mortal men in whom there is no help.
Take the breath, they return to clay
and their plans that the day come to nothing.

(Cantor) He is happy who is helped by Jacob’s God,
whose hope is in the Lord his God.
Who alone made heaven and earth,
Tte seas and all they contain.


(All) It is he who keeps the faith for ever,
who is just to those who are oppressed.
It is he who gives bread to the hungry,
the Lord, who sets prisoners free.

(Cantor) The Lord who gives sight to the blind,
who raises up those who are bowed down,
the Lord, who protects the stranger
and upholds the widow and orphan.


(All) It is the Lord who loves the just
but thwarts the path of the wicked.
The Lord will reign for ever,
Zion’s God, from age to age.   Glory…

(All) I will praise my God all days of my life.


Thursday (Week 4)
Psalm 143:1-11
(Leader) At daybreak, be merciful to me, O Lord.

(Cantor) Lord, listen to my prayer:
turn your ear to my appeal.
+  You are faithful, you are just; give answer.
Do not call your servant to judgment
for no one is just in your sight.




(All) The enemy pursues my soul;
he has crushed my life to the ground;
he has made me dwell in darkness
like the dead, long forgotten.
Therefore my spirit fails;
my heart is numb within me.

(Cantor) I remember the days that are past;
I ponder all your works.
+  I muse on what your hand has wrought
and to you I stretch out my hands.
Like a parched land my soul thirsts for you.


(All) Lord, make haste and answer;
for my spirit fails within me,
do not hide your face
lest I become like those in grave.

(Cantor) In the morning let me know your love.
For I put my trust in you.
Make me know the way I should walk:
to you I lift up my soul.


(All) Rescue me, Lord, from my enemies;
I have fled to you for refuge.
Teach me to do your will
for you, O God, are my God.
Let your good spirit guide me
in ways that are level and smooth.

(Cantor) For your name’s sake, Lord, save my life;
in your justice save my soul from distress.  Glory…

(All) At daybreak, be merciful to me, O Lord.



Psalm 147;1-11
(Leader) Let us joyfully praise the Lord our God.

+  (Cantor) Praise the Lord for he is good;
sing to our God for he is loving:
to him our praise is due.

(All) The Lord builds up Jerusalem
and brings back Israel’s exiles,
he heals the broken-hearted,
he binds up all their wounds.
He fixes the number of the stars;
he calls each one by name.

(Cantor) Our Lord is great and Almighty;
his wisdom can never be measured.
The Lord raises the lowly;
he humbles the wicked to the dust.
O sing to the Lord, giving thanks;
sing psalms to our God with the harp.


(All) He covers the heavens with clouds;
he prepares the rain for the earth,
making mountains sprout with grass
and with plants to serve man’s needs.
He provides the beasts with their food.
And young ravens that call upon him.

(Cantor) His delight is not in horses
Nor his pleasure in warriors’ strength.
The Lord delights in those who revere him,
In those who wait for his love.  Glory…

(All) Let us joyfully praise the Lord our God.


Friday (Week 4)
Psalm 147:12-20
(Leader) Create a clean heart in me, O God; renew in me a steadfast spirit.

(Cantor) Have mercy on me, God, in your kindness.
In you compassion blot out my offense.
O wash me more and more from my guilt
and cleanse me from my sin.


(All) My offenses truly I know them;
my sin is always before me.
Against you, you alone, have I sinned;
what is evil in your sight I have done.

(Cantor) That you may be justified when you give sentence
and be without reproach when you judge.
O see, in guilt I was born,
a sinner was I conceived.

(All) Indeed you love truth in the heart;
then in the secret of my heart teach me wisdom.
O purify me, then I shall be clean;
O wash me, I shall be whiter than snow.

(Cantor) Make me hear rejoicing and gladness,
that the bones you have crushed may revive.
From my sins turn away your face
and blot out all my guilt.


(All) A pure heart create for me, O God,
put a steadfast spirit within me,
Do not cast me away from your presence,
nor deprive me of your holy spirit.

(Cantor) Give me again the joy of your help;
with a spirit of fervor sustain me,
that I may teach transgressors your ways
and sinners may return to you.


(All) O rescue me, God, my helper,
and my tongue shall ring out your goodness.
O Lord, open my lips
and my mouth shall declare your praise.

(Cantor) For in sacrifice you take no delight,
burnt offering from me you would refuse,
my sacrifice, a contrite spirit.
A humbled, contrite heart you will not spurn.
